Using held-out feature evaluation, we assess whether cognitively induced features generalize beyond the data used for discovery. Our results indicate that, in our evaluated setting, reasoning control is associated with improvements in quality and efficiency of LLM-based feature discovery.

</details>


### [130] [To See the Unseen: on the Generalization Ability of Transformers in Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21632)
*Nevena Lazić,Liam Fowl,András György,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现解码器仅Transformer模型在处理训练时未见过的变量名时存在表示崩溃问题，导致未见过token的未嵌入向量坍缩到几乎相同的向量。通过架构改进、数据多样性和冻结/重置嵌入等技术组合，成功实现了对未见token的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，解码器仅Transformer模型在处理训练时未见过的变量名时无法泛化，主要原因是复制或生成未见token的困难。作者旨在深入探究这一限制的根本原因，特别是寻找除token复制困难之外的其他机制性解释。

Method: 采用理论分析与实证验证相结合的方法。首先从理论上分析未见过token的表示崩溃现象，即未嵌入向量在训练过程中坍缩到几乎相同的向量。然后基于此发现设计解决方案，包括小型架构改进以促进复制、增加数据多样性、以及冻结或重置未嵌入参数。在命题逻辑推理问题上进行受控实验验证，并进一步在Gemma 3系列开源模型中验证发现。

Result: 证明了未见过token的未嵌入崩溃是导致模型无法区分多个未见变量的关键原因，尤其在嵌入和未嵌入参数共享时更为严重。所提出的技术组合成功实现了对未见token的泛化。在Gemma 3模型的99个保留token中观察到类似的嵌入崩溃现象，且这些token的关联嵌入对微调应用是不良初始化。

Conclusion: 表示崩溃是Transformer模型符号推理泛化失败的重要机制性原因，该发现解释了"主动遗忘"等启发式干预的有效性。研究成果为改进模型的符号推理能力提供了新的理论基础和实用技术方案，对模型设计和训练策略具有指导意义。

Abstract: We investigate the ability of decoder-only transformer models to perform abstract symbolic reasoning; specifically solving propositional logic reasoning problems given in-context. Previous work demonstrated that models fail to generalize to problems involving variable names that were not observed during training, and it was shown that one reason behind this is the difficulty of copying (or generating) unseen tokens. We show both theoretically and empirically that a particular representational collapse also has a crucial role: the unembeddings (last-layer weights) of unseen tokens collapse to nearly the same vector during training. The collapse makes distinguishing multiple unseen variables difficult for the model (especially when the embedding and unembedding parameters are shared), and provides a mechanistic explanation for the effectiveness of existing heuristic interventions like "active forgetting", which periodically reset the token (un)embeddings. Based on these observations, we devise a combination of techniques, involving a small architecture change facilitating copying, data diversity, and freezing or resetting (un)embeddings, that achieves generalization to unseen tokens. We support our claims with extensive controlled experiments on propositional logic reasoning problems. Beyond synthetic experiments, we also observe evidence of (un)embedding collapse in the open-weight models in the Gemma 3 family, which includes 99 unused tokens reserved for downstream use. Empirically we find that the correlated embeddings of these tokens are a poor initialization for finetuning applications.

</details>


### [131] [GS-Quant: Granular Semantic and Generative Structural Quantization for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21649)
*Qizhuo Xie,Yunhui Liu,Yu Xing,Qianzi Hou,Xudong Jin,Tao Zheng,Tieke He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GS-Quant框架，通过生成语义连贯且结构分层的离散代码来解决知识图谱补全中图嵌入与大语言模型之间的模态差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于量化的方法通常将量化视为扁平的数值压缩，导致生成的离散代码语义纠缠，无法反映人类推理的层次特性，难以有效弥合连续图嵌入与离散LLM标记之间的模态差距。

Method: 提出GS-Quant框架，包含粒度语义增强模块和生成式结构重构模块。前者将层次知识注入码本，确保早期代码捕获全局语义类别，后期代码细化具体属性；后者对代码序列施加因果依赖，将独立离散单元转换为结构化语义描述符，并扩展LLM词汇表。

Result: 实验结果表明，GS-Quant在多项任务上显著优于现有的基于文本和基于嵌入的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过生成遵循语言由粗到细逻辑的结构化离散代码，GS-Quant成功实现了图结构与自然语言的同构推理，为知识图谱与大语言模型的融合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown immense potential in Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC), yet bridging the modality gap between continuous graph embeddings and discrete LLM tokens remains a critical challenge. While recent quantization-based approaches attempt to align these modalities, they typically treat quantization as flat numerical compression, resulting in semantically entangled codes that fail to mirror the hierarchical nature of human reasoning. In this paper, we propose GS-Quant, a novel framework that generates semantically coherent and structurally stratified discrete codes for KG entities. Unlike prior methods, GS-Quant is grounded in the insight that entity representations should follow a linguistic coarse-to-fine logic. We introduce a Granular Semantic Enhancement module that injects hierarchical knowledge into the codebook, ensuring that earlier codes capture global semantic categories while later codes refine specific attributes. Furthermore, a Generative Structural Reconstruction module imposes causal dependencies on the code sequence, transforming independent discrete units into structured semantic descriptors. By expanding the LLM vocabulary with these learned codes, we enable the model to reason over graph structures isomorphically to natural language generation. Experimental results demonstrate that GS-Quant significantly outperforms existing text-based and embedding-based baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mikumifa/GS-Quant.

</details>


### [132] [Enabling and Inhibitory Pathways of University Students' Willingness to Disclose AI Use: A Cognition-Affect-Conation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21733)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动（CAC）框架，揭示了心理安全感和评价顾虑是影响大学生披露AI使用意愿的双重心理机制，强调了支持性制度环境和清晰指导的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在高等教育中的深度融合，学生在报告AI辅助工作时的透明度问题日益凸显。现有研究缺乏对学生披露AI使用意愿背后的心理机制的深入理解，亟需探索影响学生透明度报告的关键因素及其作用路径。

Method: 采用顺序解释性混合研究设计。定量阶段收集546名大学生的调查数据，运用结构方程模型分析认知感知、情感反应与披露意愿之间的关系。定性阶段对22名学生进行半结构化访谈，进一步解释定量研究结果。

Result: 心理安全感显著提高学生披露AI使用的意愿，并受到感知公平、感知教师支持和感知组织支持的积极塑造。相反，评价顾虑降低披露意愿和心理安全感，并因感知污名、感知不确定性和隐私顾虑而增强。定性研究进一步揭示，制度清晰度和支持性教学实践促进开放态度，而政策模糊和对负面评价的恐惧导致学生采取谨慎或策略性披露行为。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了促进性和抑制性心理机制在塑造AI使用披露中的双重作用，强调了支持性制度环境和清晰指导对于促进高等教育中负责任AI透明度的关键意义。

Abstract: The increasing integration of artificial intelligence (AI) in higher education has raised important questions regarding students' transparency in reporting AI-assisted work. This study investigates the psychological mechanisms underlying university students' willingness to disclose AI use by applying the Cognition--Affect--Conation (CAC) framework. A sequential explanatory mixed-methods design was employed. In the quantitative phase, survey data were collected from 546 university students and analysed using structural equation modelling to examine the relationships among cognitive perceptions, affective responses, and disclosure intention. In the qualitative phase, semi-structured interviews with 22 students were conducted to further interpret the quantitative findings. The results indicate that psychological safety significantly increases students' willingness to disclose AI use and is positively shaped by perceived fairness, perceived teacher support, and perceived organisational support. Conversely, evaluation apprehension reduces disclosure intention and psychological safety, and is strengthened by perceived stigma, perceived uncertainty, and privacy concern. Qualitative findings further reveal that institutional clarity and supportive instructional practices encourage openness, whereas policy ambiguity and fear of negative evaluation often lead students to adopt cautious or strategic disclosure practices. Overall, the study highlights the dual role of enabling and inhibitory psychological mechanisms in shaping AI-use disclosure and underscores the importance of supportive institutional environments and clear guidance for promoting responsible AI transparency in higher education.

</details>


### [133] [Bridging the Training-Deployment Gap: Gated Encoding and Multi-Scale Refinement for Efficient Quantization-Aware Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21743)
*Dat To-Thanh,Nghia Nguyen-Trong,Hoang Vo,Hieu Bui-Minh,Tinh-Anh Nguyen-Nhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向移动端部署的高效图像增强模型，通过分层网络架构和量化感知训练（QAT）解决了低精度格式转换导致的性能下降问题，在保持低计算开销的同时实现了高保真视觉输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的移动端图像增强深度学习模型在转换为低精度格式部署时会出现显著的性能下降，存在训练与部署环境不匹配的问题，难以同时满足高质量输出和移动设备所需的快速处理速度。

Method: 提出一种专为移动端部署设计的高效图像增强模型，采用包含门控编码器模块和多尺度细化的分层网络架构来保留细粒度视觉特征；引入量化感知训练（QAT）在训练过程中模拟低精度表示的影响，使网络能够适应量化并避免后训练量化（PTQ）带来的典型质量下降。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够产生高保真的视觉输出，同时保持标准移动设备实际应用所需的低计算开销，有效解决了训练-部署不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 通过分层网络架构与量化感知训练的结合，成功实现了面向移动端的高效图像增强，为移动设备上的高质量图像处理提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Image enhancement models for mobile devices often struggle to balance high output quality with the fast processing speeds required by mobile hardware. While recent deep learning models can enhance low-quality mobile photos into high-quality images, their performance is often degraded when converted to lower-precision formats for actual use on mobile phones. To address this training-deployment mismatch, we propose an efficient image enhancement model designed specifically for mobile deployment. Our approach uses a hierarchical network architecture with gated encoder blocks and multiscale refinement to preserve fine-grained visual features. Moreover, we incorporate Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) to simulate the effects of low-precision representation during the training process. This allows the network to adapt and prevents the typical drop in quality seen with standard post-training quantization (PTQ). Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method produces high-fidelity visual output while maintaining the low computational overhead needed for practical use on standard mobile devices. The code will be available at https://github.com/GenAI4E/QATIE.git.

</details>


### [134] [Thinking with Reasoning Skills: Fewer Tokens, More Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21764)
*Guangxiang Zhao,Qilong Shi,Xusen Xiao,Xiangzheng Zhang,Tong Yang,Lin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种技能检索与复用机制，通过存储和调用可复用的推理技能，在减少推理token数量的同时提升了编码和数学推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理大语言模型在解决新问题时通常采用“从零开始推理”的模式，在冗长的中间推理过程（如思维链）中消耗大量token，导致计算成本高昂且存在重复探索的问题。

Method: 提出了一种新范式，通过总结和存储从大量推理和试错探索中提炼出的可复用推理技能，在推理时检索相关技能来指导模型推理，从而避免冗余路径并聚焦于有效的解决方案。

Result: 在编码和数学推理任务上的评估表明，该方法显著减少了推理所需的token数量，同时提升了整体性能，有效降低了单次请求成本。

Conclusion: 该方法通过技能复用打破了传统的从零推理范式，在提升推理效率和性能方面展现出显著优势，具有重要的实际应用和经济价值。

Abstract: Reasoning LLMs often spend substantial tokens on long intermediate reasoning traces (e.g., chain-of-thought) when solving new problems. We propose to summarize and store reusable reasoning skills distilled from extensive deliberation and trial-and-error exploration, and to retrieve these skills at inference time to guide future reasoning. Unlike the prevailing \emph{reasoning from scratch} paradigm, our approach first recalls relevant skills for each query, helping the model avoid redundant detours and focus on effective solution paths. We evaluate our method on coding and mathematical reasoning tasks, and find that it significantly reduces reasoning tokens while improving overall performance. The resulting lower per-request cost indicates strong practical and economic potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [135] [Who Defines "Best"? Towards Interactive, User-Defined Evaluation of LLM Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21769)
*Minji Jung,Minjae Lee,Yejin Kim,Sarang Choi,Minsuk Kahng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析LMArena基准数据集，发现现有LLM排行榜存在主题偏差和排名不稳定问题，并提出了一种交互式可视化界面，让用户自定义评估优先级，从而提高模型评估的透明度和上下文相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型（LLM）排行榜排名主要受基准设计者设定的评估优先级影响，而非反映真实用户和组织的多样化目标与约束。单一的聚合分数掩盖了模型在不同提示类型和组合下的表现差异，导致评估结果缺乏针对性。

Method: 研究者对LMArena基准数据集进行了深入分析，揭示了数据集的主题分布偏差、不同提示切片下模型排名的变化以及偏好判断的使用偏差。在此基础上，设计了一个交互式可视化界面（设计探针），允许用户通过选择和加权不同的提示切片来自定义评估优先级，并动态探索排名变化。

Result: 分析显示数据集严重偏向特定主题，模型排名在不同提示切片间存在显著差异，且偏好判断的使用模糊了其预期范围。定性研究表明，该交互式可视化界面提高了评估透明度，支持更符合特定上下文的模型评估。

Conclusion: 研究指出了现有LLM排行榜设计的局限性，并提出了一种替代性方案：通过交互式可视化工具赋予用户定义评估优先级的能力，从而实现更透明、更具上下文针对性的模型评估与比较。

Abstract: LLM leaderboards are widely used to compare models and guide deployment decisions. However, leaderboard rankings are shaped by evaluation priorities set by benchmark designers, rather than by the diverse goals and constraints of actual users and organizations. A single aggregate score often obscures how models behave across different prompt types and compositions. In this work, we conduct an in-depth analysis of the dataset used in the LMArena (formerly Chatbot Arena) benchmark and investigate this evaluation challenge by designing an interactive visualization interface as a design probe. Our analysis reveals that the dataset is heavily skewed toward certain topics, that model rankings vary across prompt slices, and that preference-based judgments are used in ways that blur their intended scope. Building on this analysis, we introduce a visualization interface that allows users to define their own evaluation priorities by selecting and weighting prompt slices and to explore how rankings change accordingly. A qualitative study suggests that this interactive approach improves transparency and supports more context-specific model evaluation, pointing toward alternative ways to design and use LLM leaderboards.

</details>


### [136] [Inferring High-Level Events from Timestamped Data: Complexity and Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21793)
*Yvon K. Awuklu,Meghyn Bienvenu,Katsumi Inoue,Vianney Jouhet,Fleur Mougin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逻辑的框架，用于从时间戳数据和背景知识中检测高层时间扩展事件，并通过约束和修复机制确保事件一致性，在肺癌用例中验证了其可行性与医学专家意见的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以从时间戳数据（如临床记录）中推断高层时间扩展事件（如疾病发作、疗程）。医学等领域需要从底层数据构建语义明确的高层事件，同时处理推理过程中的不一致性问题。

Method: 采用逻辑规则定义简单时间事件的存在与终止条件，将其组合为元事件；引入约束识别不相容事件组合，提出修复机制选择优选的一致事件集；基于回答集编程（ASP）实现原型系统。在保证多项式时间数据复杂度的前提下对框架进行了合理限制。

Result: 在肺癌用例上的评估表明：1）计算可行；2）推理结果与医学专家意见高度一致。框架虽受医疗需求驱动，但设计通用，可复用于其他领域。

Conclusion: 该框架为从时间戳数据推断高层事件提供了有效且通用的解决方案，在医疗等领域具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a novel logic-based approach to detecting high-level temporally extended events from timestamped data and background knowledge. Our framework employs logical rules to capture existence and termination conditions for simple temporal events and to combine these into meta-events. In the medical domain, for example, disease episodes and therapies are inferred from timestamped clinical observations, such as diagnoses and drug administrations stored in patient records, and can be further combined into higher-level disease events. As some incorrect events might be inferred, we use constraints to identify incompatible combinations of events and propose a repair mechanism to select preferred consistent sets of events. While reasoning in the full framework is intractable, we identify relevant restrictions that ensure polynomial-time data complexity. Our prototype system implements core components of the approach using answer set programming. An evaluation on a lung cancer use case supports the interest of the approach, both in terms of computational feasibility and positive alignment of our results with medical expert opinions. While strongly motivated by the needs of the healthcare domain, our framework is purposely generic, enabling its reuse in other areas.

</details>


### [137] [Tool Attention Is All You Need: Dynamic Tool Gating and Lazy Schema Loading for Eliminating the MCP/Tools Tax in Scalable Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21816)
*Anuj Sadani,Deepak Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tool Attention中间件机制，通过意图模式重叠评分、状态感知门控函数和两阶段懒加载模式，将MCP协议中工具令牌开销降低95%，有效上下文利用率从24%提升至91%，证明协议级效率而非原始上下文长度是可扩展智能体系统的关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型上下文协议(MCP)作为连接大语言模型代理与外部工具的标准接口，其依赖无状态、急切式模式注入机制，导致每轮对话产生约10k-60k令牌的隐性开销（MCP税或工具税）。该负载不仅膨胀键值缓存、在上下文利用率接近70%断裂点时导致推理性能下降，还将令牌预算转化为持续性运营成本，制约了可扩展智能体系统的发展。

Method: Tool Attention中间件机制将注意力范式从令牌自注意力泛化为工具门控注意力，包含三个核心组件：(i)基于句子嵌入的意图模式重叠(ISO)评分，(ii)强制前置条件和访问范围的状态感知门控函数，(iii)两阶段懒加载模式加载器，在上下文中保持紧凑摘要池，仅对top-k门控工具提升完整JSON模式。

Result: 在模拟的120工具、六服务器基准测试中（各服务器令牌数校准至真实MCP部署审计数据），Tool Attention将每轮工具令牌从47.3k降至2.4k（降低95.0%），有效上下文利用率从24%提升至91%。基于令牌计数结合已发表部署遥测数据的端到端投影显示任务成功率、延迟、成本和推理质量均有改善。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明：协议级效率而非原始上下文长度是可扩展智能体系统的瓶颈约束，Tool Attention为降低MCP协议开销提供了有效解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has become a common interface for connecting large language model (LLM) agents to external tools, but its reliance on stateless, eager schema injection imposes a hidden per-turn overhead the MCP Tax or Tools Tax that practitioner reports place between roughly 10k and 60k tokens in typical multi-server deployments. This payload inflates the key-value cache, is associated with reasoning degradation as context utilization approaches published fracture points around 70%, and turns token budgets into a recurring operational cost. We introduce Tool Attention, a middleware-layer mechanism that generalizes the "Attention Is All You Need" paradigm from self-attention over tokens to gated attention over tools. Tool Attention combines (i) an Intent Schema Overlap (ISO) score from sentence embeddings, (ii) a state-aware gating function enforcing preconditions and access scopes, and (iii) a two-phase lazy schema loader that keeps a compact summary pool in context and promotes full JSON schemas only for top-k gated tools. We evaluate on a simulated 120-tool, six-server benchmark whose per-server token counts are calibrated to public audits of real MCP deployments. In this simulation, Tool Attention directly reduces measured per-turn tool tokens by 95.0% (47.3k -> 2.4k) and raises effective context utilization (a token-ratio quantity) from 24% to 91%. End-to-end figures for task success, latency, cost, and reasoning quality are reported as projections derived from the measured token counts combined with published deployment telemetry; they are not measured on live LLM agents, and we mark projected values explicitly throughout. Taken together, the results support a simple thesis: protocol-level efficiency, not raw context length, is a binding constraint on scalable gentic systems. The code for this work is accessible at https://github.com/asadani/tool-attention

</details>


### [138] [Alignment has a Fantasia Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21827)
*Nathanael Jo,Zoe De Simone,Mitchell Gordon,Ashia Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有AI助手假设用户目标明确，忽视了用户目标尚未成形的常见情况，导致“Fantasia交互”失败。作者提出需重新思考对齐研究，让AI主动提供认知支持以帮助用户形成和修正意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统假设用户是理性的、目标明确的，但行为科学研究表明，用户往往在与AI交互时目标尚未完全形成。当系统将提示词视为完整意图表达时，可能产生看似有用但实际与用户需求不匹配的“Fantasia交互”失败。

Method: 采用跨学科方法，综合机器学习、界面设计和行为科学的见解，系统性地分析和表征Fantasia交互的机制及其失败模式。

Result: 揭示了Fantasia交互的机制和失败原因，证明了现有干预措施的不足，并提出了设计和评估能够更好帮助人类应对任务不确定性的AI系统的研究议程。

Conclusion: AI对齐研究需要根本性转变：不再将用户视为理性预言者，而是让AI系统主动提供认知支持，帮助用户在时间维度上形成和精细化其意图，这需要跨学科的协作努力。

Abstract: Modern AI assistants are trained to follow instructions, implicitly assuming that users can clearly articulate their goals and the kind of assistance they need. Decades of behavioral research, however, show that people often engage with AI systems before their goals are fully formed. When AI systems treat prompts as complete expressions of intent, they can appear to be useful or convenient, but not necessarily aligned with the users' needs. We call these failures Fantasia interactions. We argue that Fantasia interactions demand a rethinking of alignment research: rather than treating users as rational oracles, AI should provide cognitive support by actively helping users form and refine their intent through time. This requires an interdisciplinary approach that bridges machine learning, interface design, and behavioral science. We synthesize insights from these fields to characterize the mechanisms and failures of Fantasia interactions. We then show why existing interventions are insufficient, and propose a research agenda for designing and evaluating AI systems that better help humans navigate uncertainty in their tasks.

</details>


### [139] [Bounding the Black Box: A Statistical Certification Framework for AI Risk Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21854)
*Natan Levy,Gadi Perl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI监管中缺乏“可接受风险”量化定义与验证方法的问题，提出了一种借鉴航空认证的两阶段框架，通过RoMA和gRoMA工具对AI系统故障率进行统计验证并颁发可审计证书，填补了法规与工程实践之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行AI监管法规（如欧盟AI法案、NIST风险管理框架）要求高风险系统证明安全性，却未规定“可接受风险”的量化标准，也未提供验证部署系统是否达标的技术方法。随着法规进入全面执行，开发者面临合规评估却缺乏生成定量安全证据的方法论。

Method: 借鉴航空认证范式，提出两阶段框架：第一阶段由主管机构正式确定可接受故障概率δ和操作输入域ε（具有民事责任含义的规范行为）；第二阶段利用RoMA和gRoMA统计验证工具，在无需访问模型内部、适用于任意架构的情况下，计算系统真实故障率的可审计上限并颁发证书。

Result: 验证工具能够在不依赖模型内部信息的前提下，为任意架构的AI系统计算出确定、可审计的故障率上限；所颁发的证书能够满足现有监管义务，并将责任上游转移至开发者，可与现行法律框架集成。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了监管架构中缺失的验证工具，将AI风险监管转化为可操作的工程实践，使开发者能够满足强制合规评估要求，同时为“可接受风险”提供了量化定义与验证路径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence now decides who receives a loan, who is flagged for criminal investigation, and whether an autonomous vehicle brakes in time. Governments have responded: the EU AI Act, the NIST Risk Management Framework, and the Council of Europe Convention all demand that high-risk systems demonstrate safety before deployment. Yet beneath this regulatory consensus lies a critical vacuum: none specifies what ``acceptable risk'' means in quantitative terms, and none provides a technical method for verifying that a deployed system actually meets such a threshold. The regulatory architecture is in place; the verification instrument is not.
  This gap is not theoretical. As the EU AI Act moves into full enforcement, developers face mandatory conformity assessments without established methodologies for producing quantitative safety evidence - and the systems most in need of oversight are opaque statistical inference engines that resist white-box scrutiny.
  This paper provides the missing instrument. Drawing on the aviation certification paradigm, we propose a two-stage framework that transforms AI risk regulation into engineering practice. In Stage One, a competent authority formally fixes an acceptable failure probability $δ$ and an operational input domain $\varepsilon$ - a normative act with direct civil liability implications. In Stage Two, the RoMA and gRoMA statistical verification tools compute a definitive, auditable upper bound on the system's true failure rate, requiring no access to model internals and scaling to arbitrary architectures. We demonstrate how this certificate satisfies existing regulatory obligations, shifts accountability upstream to developers, and integrates with the legal frameworks that exist today.

</details>


### [140] [Nemobot Games: Crafting Strategic AI Gaming Agents for Interactive Learning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21896)
*Chee Wei Tan,Yuchen Wang,Shangxin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于大语言模型（LLM）的AI游戏编程新范式，并介绍了交互式工程环境Nemobot，支持用户创建、定制和部署LLM驱动的游戏智能体，实现跨四类游戏的策略能力，向自编程AI目标迈进一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在扩展并操作化Claude Shannon的游戏机器分类法，利用大语言模型的能力构建一个可交互的工程环境，使用户能够实验、生成和微调策略型游戏智能体，推动自编程AI的发展。

Method: 提出Nemobot交互式智能体工程环境，集成LLM聊天机器人，针对四类游戏分别采用：字典类游戏压缩状态-动作映射以快速适应；可严格求解类游戏使用数学推理计算最优策略并生成人类可读解释；启发式类游戏结合经典minimax算法与众包数据综合策略；学习类游戏采用带人类反馈的强化学习与自我批评迭代优化策略。环境支持工具增强生成和策略智能体微调。

Result: Nemobot能够支持从策略游戏到角色扮演游戏等多种类型，LLM智能体在四类游戏中展现出策略能力，用户可在可编程环境中进行实验，验证了AI智能体通过整合众包学习与人类创造力实现自我逻辑迭代优化的可行性。

Conclusion: 该范式展示了AI智能体如何实现某种形式的自编程，代表了向长期目标——自编程AI——迈进的一步。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new paradigm for AI game programming, leveraging large language models (LLMs) to extend and operationalize Claude Shannon's taxonomy of game-playing machines. Central to this paradigm is Nemobot, an interactive agentic engineering environment that enables users to create, customize, and deploy LLM-powered game agents while actively engaging with AI-driven strategies. The LLM-based chatbot, integrated within Nemobot, demonstrates its capabilities across four distinct classes of games. For dictionary-based games, it compresses state-action mappings into efficient, generalized models for rapid adaptability. In rigorously solvable games, it employs mathematical reasoning to compute optimal strategies and generates human-readable explanations for its decisions. For heuristic-based games, it synthesizes strategies by combining insights from classical minimax algorithms (see, e.g., shannon1950chess) with crowd-sourced data. Finally, in learning-based games, it utilizes reinforcement learning with human feedback and self-critique to iteratively refine strategies through trial-and-error and imitation learning. Nemobot amplifies this framework by offering a programmable environment where users can experiment with tool-augmented generation and fine-tuning of strategic game agents. From strategic games to role-playing games, Nemobot demonstrates how AI agents can achieve a form of self-programming by integrating crowdsourced learning and human creativity to iteratively refine their own logic. This represents a step toward the long-term goal of self-programming AI.

</details>


### [141] [From Research Question to Scientific Workflow: Leveraging Agentic AI for Science Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21910)
*Bartosz Balis,Michal Orzechowski,Piotr Kica,Michal Dygas,Michal Kuszewski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种智能体架构，通过分层设计将大语言模型的非确定性限制在意图提取阶段，实现了从自然语言到可复现科学工作流的自动化转换，显著提升了准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学工作流系统虽然能够自动化执行调度、容错和资源管理，但无法自动处理执行前的语义转换。科学家仍需手动将研究问题转换为工作流规范，这需要同时具备领域知识和基础设施专业知识，存在明显的效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出三层智能体架构：语义层利用大语言模型将自然语言解释为结构化意图；确定性层通过验证生成器生成可复现的工作流DAG；知识层由领域专家编写“技能”（markdown文档），编码词汇映射、参数约束和优化策略。这种分解将LLM的非确定性限制在意图提取阶段，确保相同意图始终产生相同工作流。

Result: 在1000基因组群体遗传学工作流和运行于Kubernetes的Hyperflow WMS上进行评估。在150个查询的消融研究中，技能将完整匹配意图准确率从44%提升至83%；技能驱动的延迟工作流生成减少了92%的数据传输；端到端管道在Kubernetes上完成查询的LLM开销低于15秒，每次查询成本低于0.001美元。

Conclusion: 该架构成功弥合了自然语言研究问题与工作流规范之间的鸿沟，通过将领域知识与确定性生成相结合，在保证可复现性的同时显著提升了自动化程度和效率，为科学工作流的智能化构建提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific workflow systems automate execution -- scheduling, fault tolerance, resource management -- but not the semantic translation that precedes it. Scientists still manually convert research questions into workflow specifications, a task requiring both domain knowledge and infrastructure expertise. We propose an agentic architecture that closes this gap through three layers: an LLM interprets natural language into structured intents (semantic layer); validated generators produce reproducible workflow DAGs (deterministic layer); and domain experts author ``Skills'': markdown documents encoding vocabulary mappings, parameter constraints, and optimization strategies (knowledge layer). This decomposition confines LLM non-determinism to intent extraction: identical intents always yield identical workflows. We implement and evaluate the architecture on the 1000 Genomes population genetics workflow and Hyperflow WMS running on Kubernetes. In an ablation study on 150 queries, Skills raise full-match intent accuracy from 44% to 83%; skill-driven deferred workflow generation reduces data transfer by 92\%; and the end-to-end pipeline completes queries on Kubernetes with LLM overhead below 15 seconds and cost under $0.001 per query.

</details>


### [142] [Handbook of Rough Set Extensions and Uncertainty Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19794)
*Takaaki Fujita,Florentin Smarandache*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本书是粗糙集模型的系统导览，按粒化机制和不确定性语义对主要范式及其扩展进行分类综述，而非专注于特征约简或规则归纳算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙集理论通过由不可分辨性或粒化关系诱导的下近似和上近似来对目标概念进行建模，捕捉由有限观测分辨率导致的模糊性。本书旨在为该领域提供一张“模型地图”，系统梳理粗糙集的主要范式及其扩展路径，阐明各模型的形式与边界区域解释，而非深入开发单一算法流程。

Method: 以“模型地图”为定位，按两条主线组织代表性变体：（i）底层粒化机制（等价关系、容差关系、覆盖、邻域、概率近似等）；（ii）数据与关系的不确定性语义（经典、模糊、直觉模糊、中智、多重设置等）。全书通过小规模示例阐释建模意图，并在分类与决策支持等场景中说明典型用法；明确将特征约简与规则归纳作为驱动性应用与研究入口，而非本书主要目标。

Result: 系统呈现了粗糙集主要范式及其扩展的分类与定位，阐明不同粒化机制与不确定性语义如何改变近似形式与边界区域解释；通过示例澄清建模意图与典型用例，为读者提供从经典到多类不确定性设置的清晰全景与对比框架。

Conclusion: 本书的主旨是系统性、连贯地综述与定位粗糙集模型及其扩展，为研究者提供全景式导航与对比框架；特征约简与规则归纳虽为核心应用，但在本书中主要作为驱动性应用与研究入口，而非主要目标。

Abstract: Rough set theory models uncertainty by approximating target concepts through lower and upper sets induced by indiscernibility, or more generally, by granulation relations in data tables. This perspective captures vagueness caused by limited observational resolution and supports set-theoretic reasoning about what can be determined with certainty and what remains only possible. This book is written as a map of models. Rather than developing a single algorithmic pipeline in depth, it provides a systematic survey of the main rough set paradigms and their extension routes. More specifically, representative variants are organized according to (i) the underlying granulation mechanism, such as equivalence-based, tolerance-based, covering-based, neighborhood-based, and probabilistic approximations, and (ii) the uncertainty semantics attached to data and relations, such as crisp, fuzzy, intuitionistic fuzzy, neutrosophic, and plithogenic settings. The book also explains how each choice changes the form of approximations and the interpretation of boundary regions. Throughout the book, small illustrative examples are used to clarify modeling intent and typical use cases in classification and decision support. Finally, an important clarification of scope should be noted. Since the main purpose of this book is to provide a map of models, the Abstract and Introduction should not lead readers to expect that feature reduction and rule induction are primary objectives. Although these topics are central in the rough set literature, they are treated here mainly as motivating applications and as entry points to the broader research landscape. The principal aim of the book is to survey and position rough set models and their extensions in a systematic and coherent manner.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [AGNT2: Autonomous Agent Economies on Interaction-Optimized Layer 2 Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21129)
*Anbang Ruan,Xing Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出AGNT2，一个专为链上AI代理与微服务协调设计的三层执行架构，旨在解决现有L2方案无法高效处理高频、语义丰富服务调用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链Layer 2方案（如Optimism、Arbitrum、zkSync）针对人类发起的金融交易进行优化，但无法有效支持自主AI代理之间的高频、语义丰富的服务调用。现有链将这些交互视为通用调用数据，迫使身份、托管、依赖排序和会话状态在执行层之上以错误的成本点进行编码。

Method: AGNT2采用三层架构：(1) Sidecar部署模式，无需修改应用代码即可将任何Docker容器转化为链上代理；(2) 三层网络结构——Layer Top为P2P状态通道（<100ms，每对目标1K-5K TPS，整体设计目标10M+ TPS），Layer Core为依赖感知的排序Rollup（500ms-2s，目标300K-500K TPS），Layer Root为锚定任意EVM L1的计算欺诈证明结算层；(3) 代理原生执行环境与交互Trie，使服务调用、身份、声誉、能力和会话上下文成为一等协议对象。

Result: 仿真与分析建模支持该架构设计，原型测量验证了部分组件。然而目前尚无端到端的Layer Core实现，实际部署受数据可用性（DA）吞吐量限制，当前仅能达到约10K-100K TPS，与目标上限存在约100倍差距。

Conclusion: AGNT2论证了代理经济需要专用的执行层，而非将通用链改用于代理场景，同时指出了DA带宽差距是制约性能的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Current blockchain Layer 2 solutions, including Optimism, Arbitrum, zkSync, and their derivatives, optimize for human-initiated financial transactions. Autonomous AI agents instead generate high-frequency, semantically rich service invocations among mutually untrusting principals. Existing chains treat those interactions as generic calldata, forcing identity, escrow, dependency ordering, and session state to be encoded above the execution layer at the wrong cost point. We present AGNT2, a three-tier stack purpose-built for agent and microservice coordination on-chain. AGNT2 combines: (1) a sidecar deployment pattern that turns any Docker container into an on-chain agent without application-code modification; (2) Layer Top P2P state channels for established bilateral pairs (<100 ms, rough design target 1K-5K TPS per pair, 10M+ aggregate TPS design envelope under endpoint-resource limits), Layer Core as a dependency-aware sequenced rollup for first-contact and multi-party interactions (500 ms-2 s, 300K-500K TPS design target), and Layer Root settlement with computational fraud proofs anchored to any EVM L1; and (3) an agent-native execution environment plus interaction trie that make service invocation, identity, reputation, capabilities, and session context first-class protocol objects. This paper focuses on the execution-layer systems problem: sequencing, state, settlement, and the data-availability (DA) bandwidth gap that bounds all three. Simulation and analytical modeling support the architecture, and prototype measurements validate selected components, but no end-to-end Layer Core implementation exists yet. Practical deployment is currently constrained to roughly 10K-100K TPS by DA throughput, leaving a ~100x gap at the target ceiling. AGNT2 argues that the agent economy requires a dedicated execution layer rather than a general-purpose chain repurposed for agents.

</details>


### [144] [Role of diversity in team performance: the case of missing expertise, an agent based simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21328)
*Tamás Kiss*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究开发了基于代理的模型来研究管理团队功能性多样性，发现个人内功能多样性（IFD）和主导功能多样性（DFD）对团队绩效的影响取决于情境因素，并指出需要第三个衡量指标来全面解释实证发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于实验的社会系统研究虽然收集了大量数据，但往往只分析测量变量分布的前一两个模式，且难以分析情境效应。在功能性多样性研究中，管理团队由包含个体管理者和团队管理者能力复杂分布的指标来描述，因此需要研究隐藏分布的影响以及功能性多样性组成对团队沟通和绩效的影响。

Method: 开发了基于代理的模型（agent-based model），并进行了一系列仿真实验，以研究个人内功能多样性（IFD）和主导功能多样性（DFD）在不同情境下对团队绩效和沟通的影响。

Result: 仿真结果表明，根据情境（如互动代理间的沟通方案或其功能组成）的不同，个人内功能多样性（IFD）和主导功能多样性（DFD）可能会增强或降低代理间的绩效和沟通。此外，仿真结果还表明，除了IFD和DFD之外，还需要第三个衡量团队整体专业知识的指标，以全面解释实证发现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了情境因素在功能性多样性影响团队绩效中的关键作用，表明IFD和DFD的影响具有情境依赖性，并提出需要第三个衡量指标来更全面地理解和解释管理团队功能性多样性对团队绩效的影响。

Abstract: Theory and empirical research on management teams' influence on firm performance have witnessed continuous development, and by now incorporate numerous details. Classic, experiment-based studies examining social systems collect vast amount of data, but often times investigate only the first one or two modes of the distribution of measured variables, and experience difficulty in analyzing the effect of context. For example, in functional diversity research, management teams are described by measures incorporating complex distributions of capabilities of individual managers and teams of managers. To investigate the effect of hidden distributions, and the effect of functional diversity composition on team communication and performance, we developed an agent-based model, and conducted a series of simulation experiments. Modeling results show that depending on the context, such as communication scheme among interacting agents, or their functional composition, intrapersonal functional diversity (IFD), and dominant function diversity (DFD) might enhance or reduce performance and communication among agents. Furthermore, simulation results also suggest that a third measure is required alongside IFD and DFD capturing the aggregate expertise of the team to comprehensively account for empirical findings.

</details>


### [145] [Architectures for Robust Self-Organizing Energy Systems under Information and Control Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21529)
*Emilie Frost,Astrid Nieße*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了适用于信息与动作受限环境的观察者/控制器架构变体，用于实现信息物理能源系统的受控自组织，以提升系统对抗网络攻击等扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控自组织是提升基于智能体的信息物理能源系统鲁棒性的有效途径，但在实际部署时，观察者与控制器面临本地分布式能源隐私保护、监管约束及数据交换要求所带来的信息获取与动作执行限制。

Method: 提出多种观察者/控制器架构变体，明确纳入信息访问受限与动作受限的约束，并在不同架构下评估与比较可行的控制器动作。

Result: 评估结果表明，合理设计的观察者/控制器架构能够在受限条件下有效支持受控自组织，不同架构对系统响应能力的影响存在差异。

Conclusion: 在设计基于智能体的信息物理能源系统时，必须充分考虑受限条件下的观察者/控制器架构，这对确保系统在真实场景中的鲁棒性至关重要。

Abstract: Applying the concept of controlled self-organization in agent-based Cyber-Physical Energy Systems (CPES) is a promising approach to ensure system robustness. By introducing an observer/controller architecture to the system, this concept allows for self-organization while still enabling intervention when disturbances occur. Thus, it is possible to respond to effects of cyber attacks, a major threat to current energy systems. However, when implementing an observer to monitor the system and a controller to execute actions for controlled self-organization in CPES, it is essential to take into account restrictions on information and actions resulting from the privacy of local distributed energy resources, regulatory constraints, and data exchange requirements. For this reason, this paper presents architecture variants for the observer and controller that take into account restrictions on access to information and limited actions. In addition, it evaluates possible controller actions in various architectures. The results underscore the importance of considering observer/controller architectures when designing agent-based systems to ensure their robustness for real-world applications.

</details>


### [146] [Agentic AI-Enabled Framework for Thermal Comfort and Building Energy Assessment in Tropical Urban Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21787)
*Po-Yen Lai,Xinyu Yang,Derrick Low,Huizhe Liu,Jian Cheng Wong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个结合大语言模型与轻量级物理模型的智能推理框架，用于新加坡城市热岛效应缓解与建筑能耗优化，实现了从设计意图到定量评估的闭环自动化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对新加坡面临的城市热岛效应加剧和建筑能源需求增长的双重挑战，传统物理模型计算成本高昂且缺乏灵活性，亟需一种能够理解设计意图、快速评估并支持决策的智能化方法。

Method: 提出一种代理式AI推理框架，通过提示定制化使大语言模型解读城市设计任务、提取相关政策，并激活相应的轻量级物理模型进行评估。轻量级物理模型基于核心热力学和气流原理，简化传统模型以在保证预测精度（如建筑表面温度、地面辐射热、气流条件、PET热舒适指数、建筑能耗等）的同时大幅降低计算时间。

Result: 该框架使用户能够高效探索多种气候适应性建筑表面策略（如绿色立面、冷涂料应用），验证了这些策略在提升热舒适度、降低墙体得热及减少能源需求方面的有效性。系统成功实现了LLM的自主推理能力与轻量级物理模型的快速定量评估的有效结合。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将大语言模型的语义理解与推理能力同轻量级物理模型的快速计算相结合的可行性，为可持续城市设计、室内外环境整合及气候适应规划提供了跨学科应用潜力。开源代码和数据促进了方法的透明度与可复现性。

Abstract: In response to the urban heat island effects and building energy demands in Singapore, this study proposes an agentic AI-enabled reasoning framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) with lightweight physics-based models. Through prompt customization, the LLMs interpret urban design tasks, extract relevant policies, and activate appropriate physics-based models for evaluation, forming a closed-loop reasoning-action process. These lightweight physics-based models leverage core thermal and airflow principles, streamlining conventional models to reduce computational time while predicting microclimate variables, such as building surface temperature, ground radiant heat, and airflow conditions, thereby enabling the estimation of thermal comfort indices, e.g., physiological equivalent temperature (PET), and building energy usage. This framework allows users to explore a variety of climate-resilient building surface strategies, e.g., green façades and cool paint applications, that improve thermal comfort while reducing wall heat gain and energy demand. By combining the autonomous reasoning capacity of LLMs with the rapid quantitative evaluation of lightweight physics-based models, the proposed system demonstrates potential for cross-disciplinary applications in sustainable urban design, indoor-outdoor environmental integration, and climate adaptation planning. The source code and data used in this study are available at: https://github.com/PgUpDn/urban-cooling-agent.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [StarLoc: Pinpointing Transmitting LEO Satellites from a Single Passive Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21147)
*Ishani Janveja,Jida Zhang,Emerson Sie,Deepak Vasisht*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StarLoc系统，利用轨道建模和干涉测量技术，仅需平面内的三根天线即可实现LEO卫星的3D定位，角度误差0.7°，测距误差5km。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星的3D定位是频谱管理、轨道测定和GPS备份等应用中的新兴重要问题，现有方法难以高效解决该挑战。

Method: 利用卫星运动受轨道动力学约束从而在3D空间中沿2D流形运动的特性，降低运动自由度；结合轨道建模与新型干涉测量3D到达角估计技术，使用平面内的三根天线实现定位。

Result: 在81颗Starlink卫星的信号传输实验中，StarLoc实现了0.7度的3D角度估计误差和5km的轨道距离误差。

Conclusion: StarLoc系统通过轨道动力学约束与新干涉测量技术，实现了低成本天线配置下的高精度LEO卫星3D定位，为相关应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on 3D localization of transmitting satellites in low Earth orbits (LEO). 3D localization of transmitters in low orbits is an important emerging problem for many applications such as spectrum management, orbit determination, and backup for GPS failures in orbit. We present StarLoc -- a system to geolocate transmitters in space using a combination of orbital modeling and a new interferometric 3D angle-of-arrival estimation technique. StarLoc's design relies on a unique insight -- the motion of satellites is governed by orbital dynamics and is therefore along a 2D manifold in a 3D space. This reduces the degrees of freedom in satellite motion and allows us to 3D-locate and track a satellite with just three antennas in a 2D plane. We evaluate the system using signal transmissions from 81 Starlink satellites. Our results show that StarLoc can estimate the 3D-angle of a satellite within 0.7 degrees and the orbital range within 5 km. Our dataset and implementation are available at: https://connectedsystemslab.github.io/starloc.

</details>


### [148] [SPAC: Automating FPGA-based Network Switches with Protocol Adaptive Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21881)
*Guoyu Li,Yang Cao,Lucas H L Ng,Alexander Charlton,Qianzhou Wang,Will Punter,Philippos Papaphilippou,Ce Guo,Hongxiang Fan,Wayne Luk,Saman Amarasinghe,Ajay Brahmakshatriya*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPAC框架，通过领域特定语言、模块化组件库和追踪感知的设计空间探索引擎，自动生成针对特定协议和流量模式优化的FPGA网络交换机，显著降低资源消耗和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着新兴应用的网络需求分化，延迟敏感型服务需要极低的逻辑延迟，而大规模训练和集合通信需要持续的线速吞吐量进行同步批量传输。这种分化迫切需要针对专用协议和特定应用流量模式定制的网络交换机。

Method: SPAC提出了一个统一工作流：1）用于协议-架构协同设计的领域特定语言（DSL）；2）基于高级综合（HLS）的模块化自适应交换机组件库；3）追踪感知的设计空间探索（DSE）引擎。通过多保真度仿真栈，在部署前快速识别帕累托最优设计。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过针对特定工作负载定制微架构和协议，SPAC生成的设计分别减少了55%的LUT使用量和53%的BRAM使用量。与固定架构方案相比，SPAC在各种任务中实现了7.8%至38.4%的延迟降低，同时保持适当的资源消耗和丢包率。

Conclusion: SPAC成功展示了领域特定适应的有效性，覆盖从延迟敏感的传感器网络、高频交易网络到超大规模数据中心网络等多种现实场景，证明了协议-架构协同优化能够显著提升FPGA网络交换机的性能和资源效率。

Abstract: With network requirements diverging across emerging applications, latency-critical services demand minimal logic delay, while hyperscale training and collectives require sustained line-rate throughput for synchronized bulk transfers. This divergence creates an urgent need for custom network switches tailored to specialized protocols and application-specific traffic patterns. This paper presents SPAC (Switch and Protocol Adaptive Customization), a novel approach that automates the generation of FPGA-based network switches co-optimized for custom protocols and application-specific traffic patterns. SPAC introduces a unified workflow with a domain-specific language (DSL) for protocol-architecture co-design, a library of modular HLS-based adaptive switch components, and a trace-aware Design Space Exploration (DSE) engine. By providing a multi-fidelity simulation stack, SPAC enables rapid identification of Pareto-optimal designs prior to deployment. We demonstrate the efficacy of the domain-specific adaptation of SPAC across a spectrum of real-world scenarios, spanning from latency-sensitive sensor and HFT networks to hyperscale datacenter fabrics. Experimental results show that by tailoring the micro-architecture and protocol to the specific workload, SPAC-generated designs reduce LUT and BRAM usage by 55% and 53%, respectively. Compared to fixed-architecture counterparts, SPAC delivers latency reductions ranging from 7.8% to 38.4% across various tasks while maintaining adequate resource consumption and packet drop rate.

</details>


### [149] [SparKV: Overhead-Aware KV Cache Loading for Efficient On-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21231)
*Hongyao Liu,Liuqun Zhai,Junyi Wang,Zhengru Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SparKV自适应KV加载框架，通过云端流式传输与本地计算协同，显著降低了端侧大模型推理的延迟与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧大语言模型推理面临硬件资源受限与预填充阶段成本高昂的双重挑战，该阶段需处理完整输入上下文以构建键值（KV）缓存。

Method: 提出了SparKV框架，结合云端KV流式传输与端侧计算。该方法对单个KV块的代价进行建模，决策其应被流式传输还是本地计算，并通过重叠两条执行路径来降低延迟。此外，针对无线连接波动和边缘资源可用性变化，系统在运行时动态优化离线生成的调度方案。

Result: 在多种数据集、LLM和边缘设备上的实验表明，SparKV将首个词元生成时间（TTFT）减少了1.3倍至5.1倍，且对回复质量影响极小；同时将每次请求的能耗降低了1.5倍至3.3倍。

Conclusion: SparKV展现了在实际端侧部署中的鲁棒性与实用性，为解决资源受限环境下的LLM高效推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Efficient inference for on-device Large Language Models (LLMs) remains challenging due to limited hardware resources and the high cost of the prefill stage, which processes the full input context to construct Key-Value (KV) caches. We present SparKV, an adaptive KV loading framework that combines cloud-based KV streaming with on-device computation. SparKV models the cost of individual KV chunks and decides whether each chunk should be streamed or computed locally, while overlapping the two execution paths to reduce latency. To handle fluctuations in wireless connectivity and edge resource availability, SparKV further refines offline-generated schedules at runtime to rebalance communication and computation costs. Experiments across diverse datasets, LLMs, and edge devices show that SparKV reduces Time-to-First-Token by 1.3$x-5.1x with negligible impact on response quality, while lowering per-request energy consumption by 1.5x to 3.3x, demonstrating its robustness and practicality for real-world on-device deployment.

</details>


### [150] [An Efficient Wireless iBCI Headstage with Adaptive ADC Sample Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21247)
*Hongyao Liu,Junyi Wang,Liuqun Zhai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种服务器驱动的无线植入式脑机接口(iBCI)头端设计，通过自适应ADC采样和锋电位检测在采集层直接降低数据量，实现了40 mW功耗降低和3.2倍的FPGA资源减少，同时保持或提升了解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线植入式脑机接口(iBCIs)在临床和日常应用中日益重要，但面临严重的功耗和数据吞吐量限制。传统的应用层压缩方法计算开销大，无法有效解决采集层的根本瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出服务器驱动的架构，实现源级效率优化。服务器学习最优的电极特定采样率向量，动态重新配置ADC硬件。该策略直接在采集层(ADC和放大器)降低数据量，而非依赖计算密集的数字化后处理。系统包含自适应ADC采样和锋电位检测功能。

Result: 在多个受试者和阵列的广泛实验中，实现了高达40 mW的功耗降低，FPGA资源利用率降低3.2倍，同时在运动和视觉任务中保持或超过了解码精度。原型系统已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 该设计为长期在体记录提供了高度实用的解决方案，通过在采集层而非后处理层优化数据量，有效解决了无线iBCI的功耗和吞吐量瓶颈问题，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Implantable Brain-Computer Interfaces (iBCIs) are increasingly pivotal in clinical and daily applications. However, wireless iBCIs face severe constraints in power consumption and data throughput. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose a wireless iBCI headstage featuring adaptive ADC sampling and spike detection. Distinguishing our design from traditional application-layer compression, we employ a server-driven architecture that achieves source-level efficiency. Specifically, the server learns an optimal, electrode-specific sample rate vector to dynamically reconfigure the ADC hardware. This strategy reduces data volume directly at the acquisition layer (ADC and amplifier) rather than relying on computationally intensive post-digitization processing. Extensive experiments across diverse subjects and arrays demonstrate a power reduction of up to 40 mW and a 3.2$\times$ decrease in FPGA resource utilization, all while maintaining or exceeding decoding accuracy in both motor and visual tasks. This design offers a highly practical solution for long-term in-vivo recording.Our prototype is open-sourced in: https://github.com/liuhongyao99cs/32-Channel-Wireless-BCI-Headstage.

</details>


### [151] [Iterative Receiver Processing at Relays in PNC-Enabled Multi-Hop Underwater Acoustic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21819)
*Gewei Zhang,Deqing Wang,Lizhao You,Xiangming Cai,Liqun Fu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于PNC使能的多跳水声OFDM网络的迭代检测与解码处理策略，包含自适应信道感知检测、校验约束软信息细化和基于叠加模型的LMMSE检测三种算法，有效解决了多径时延扩展、多普勒频移引起的ICI以及多跳错误累积问题，显著降低了误码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双向多跳水声网络中，物理层网络编码（PNC）虽能提升端到端吞吐量，但水声信道的多径时延扩展和多普勒效应引起的载波间干扰（ICI）会严重降低三节点中继配置下PNC传输的可靠性。更关键的是，错误在多个中继节点间的累积会导致多跳网络端到端误码率（BER）显著上升，亟需有效的解决方案。

Method: 基于正交频分复用（OFDM）调制，为中继节点开发了迭代检测与解码处理策略。该设计集成了三个核心算法：（i）适用于时变水声信道的自适应信道感知因子图检测算法；（ii）提高解码器到检测器信息反馈精度的校验约束软信息细化算法；（iii）基于叠加模型的线性最小均方误差（LMMSE）检测算法，作为低计算复杂度的替代方案。

Result: 大量仿真结果表明，自适应检测算法在相对速度1.5 m/s的水声信道和8 dB信噪比下实现了10^-5数量级的误码率。在台湾海峡的湖上实验和海试中，所提出的迭代接收机算法在实际水声信道条件下的误码率性能均优于基准方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的迭代接收机算法有效解决了多跳水声网络中的错误累积问题，在实际水声信道条件下表现出优异的误码率性能和鲁棒性，证明了其在实际多跳部署中的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Physical-layer network coding (PNC) can increase end-to-end throughput in bi-directional multi-hop underwater acoustic (UWA) networks. However, multipath delay spread and Doppler-induced inter-carrier interference (ICI) in UWA channels can degrade the reliability of PNC transmission in a three-node relay configuration. More critically, error accumulation across multiple relay nodes leads to a pronounced increase in the end-to-end bit error rate (BER) in multi-hop networks. To address this issue, we develop an iterative detection and decoding processing strategy for relay nodes within a PNC-enabled multi-hop UWA network based on orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) modulation. The proposed design integrates three key algorithms: (i) an adaptive channel-aware factor graph detection algorithm that is suited for time-varying UWA channels; (ii) a parity-check-constrained soft-information refinement algorithm that improves the accuracy of the information feedback from the decoder to the detector; and (iii) a linear minimum mean square error (LMMSE) detection algorithm based on a superimposed model, which offers low computational complexity as an alternative scheme. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the adaptive detection algorithm achieves BERs on the order of $10^{-5}$ at a relative velocity of 1.5 m/s UWA channel and a signal-to-noise (SNR) of 8~dB. Both lake experiments and sea trials in the Taiwan Strait confirm that the proposed iterative receiver algorithms outperform baseline schemes in terms of BER performance under practical UWA channel conditions, showing their robustness and applicability in real multi-hop deployments.

</details>

### [152] [Distributed Generative Inference of LLM at Internet Scales with Multi-Dimensional Communication Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21072)
*Jiu Chen,Shuangyan Yang,Xu Xiong,Hexiao Duan,Xinran Zhang,Jie Ren,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了BloomBee，一个互联网规模的分布式大语言模型推理框架，通过层分配、微批处理和张量卸载等多维度优化通信，在低带宽网络环境下相比现有系统吞吐量提升高达1.76倍，平均延迟降低高达43.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化的大语言模型推理能够在互联网上的异构节点间分布计算，提供了一种与传统集中式推理不同的高性能且高性价比的解决方案。然而，跨节点网络带宽较低使得通信成为主要瓶颈，限制了系统性能的进一步提升。

Method: BloomBee集成了LLM层分配、微批处理和张量卸载技术，从多个维度优化通信；将这些技术的协调形式化为优化问题并使用动态规划求解；针对低带宽网络环境定制了无损压缩和推测解码技术以减少通信开销。

Result: 在多种网络环境下的评估表明，BloomBee将服务吞吐量提升高达1.76倍，与最先进的去中心化LLM推理系统相比，平均延迟降低高达43.20%。

Conclusion: BloomBee有效解决了去中心化LLM推理中的通信瓶颈问题，显著提升了系统性能，为低带宽网络环境下的分布式推理提供了有效的解决方案，并且已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Decentralized LLM inference distributes computation among heterogeneous nodes across the internet, offering a performant and cost-efficient solution, alternative to traditional centralized inference. However, the low cross-node network bandwidth makes communication the primary bottleneck. In this paper, we introduce BloomBee, an internet-scale distributed LLM inference framework. BloomBee integrates LLM-layer assignment, micro-batching and tensor offloading to optimize communication from multiple dimensions. Additionally, BloomBee formulates the coordination of these techniques as an optimization problem and solves it using dynamic programming. BloomBee also customizes lossless compression and speculative decoding according to low-bandwidth network settings to reduce communication overhead. We evaluate BloomBee across a spectrum of network environments and show that it improves service throughput by up to 1.76x. It also reduces average latency by up to 43.20% compared to state-of-the-art decentralized LLM inference systems. BloomBee is open-sourced.

</details>


### [153] [TorchGWAS : GPU-accelerated GWAS for thousands of quantitative phenotypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21095)
*Xingzhong Zhao,Ziqian Xie,Islam,Sheikh Muhammad Saiful,Tian Xia,Chen,Cheng,Degui Zhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TorchGWAS是一个基于GPU硬件加速的高通量全基因组关联分析框架，通过复用基因型矩阵实现对大规模表型面板的高效分析，相比传统CPU方法在表型处理通量上提升了300-1700倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生物信息学流程（特别是成像和表征学习）可从单个队列生成数千至数万个定量表型。在此类场景下，逐个表型运行全基因组关联分析迅速成为计算瓶颈。现有GWAS工具虽然对单个表型分析非常有效，但未针对需要跨大表型面板复用相同基因型矩阵的表型丰富型筛选流程进行优化。

Method: 提出TorchGWAS框架，通过硬件加速实现对大表型面板的高通量关联检验。该框架提供稳定的Python和命令行工作流程，支持线性GWAS和多变量表型筛选，兼容NumPy、PLINK和BGEN基因型输入格式，可按样本标识符对齐表型和协变量表，并在内部执行协变量调整。

Result: 在包含890万个标记和23,000个样本的基准测试中，fastGWA在AMD EPYC 7763 64核CPU上每个表型需约100秒；而TorchGWAS在单个NVIDIA A100 GPU上完成2,048个表型需10分钟，完成20,480个表型需20分钟，表型处理通量提升约300至1700倍。

Conclusion: TorchGWAS使得在需要高效评估数千个定量表型的表型丰富场景下，大规模GWAS筛选变得切实可行，显著解决了大规模表型分析的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Motivation: Modern bioinformatics workflows, particularly in imaging and representation learning, can generate thousands to tens of thousands of quantitative phenotypes from a single cohort. In such settings, running genome-wide association analyses trait by trait rapidly becomes a computational bottleneck. While established GWAS tools are highly effective for individual traits, they are not optimized for phenotype-rich screening workflows in which the same genotype matrix is reused across a large phenotype panel. Results: We present TorchGWAS, a framework for high-throughput association testing of large phenotype panels through hardware acceleration. The current public release provides stable Python and command-line workflows for linear GWAS and multivariate phenotype screening, supports NumPy, PLINK, and BGEN genotype inputs, aligns phenotype and covariate tables by sample identifier, and performs covariate adjustment internally. In a benchmark with 8.9 million markers and 23,000 samples, fastGWA required approximately 100 second per phenotype on an AMD EPYC 7763 64-core CPU, whereas TorchGWAS completed 2,048 phenotypes in 10 minute and 20,480 phenotypes in 20 minutes on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, corresponding to an approximately 300- to 1700-fold increase in phenotype throughput. TorchGWAS therefore makes large-scale GWAS screening practical in phenotype-rich settings where thousands of quantitative traits must be evaluated efficiently. Availability and implementation: TorchGWAS is implemented in Python and distributed as a documented source repository at https://github.com/ZhiGroup/TorchGWAS. The current release provides a command-line interface, packaged source code, tutorials, benchmark scripts, and example workflows.

</details>


### [154] [A Cloud-Native Architecture for Human-in-Control LLM-Assisted OpenSearch in Investigative Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21125)
*Benjamin Puhani,Kai Brehmer,Malte Prieß*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向私有云部署的云原生微服务架构，将大语言模型集成到"人机协同"工作流中，用于将自然语言查询转化为OpenSearch查询语句，以解决刑事调查中非结构化证据检索的语义鸿沟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的刑事调查往往受到大量非结构化证据的阻碍，且自然语言调查意图与技术搜索逻辑之间存在语义鸿沟，这给证据检索带来了巨大挑战。

Method: 设计了一个云原生微服务架构，集成大语言模型实现"人在环中"工作流，将自然语言查询转换为语法有效的OpenSearch DSL表达式；在OpenSearch中实现了混合检索策略，结合基于BM25的词汇搜索和嵌套语义向量嵌入。

Result: 通过功能原型验证了技术可行性，展示了系统在私有云环境下的安全部署能力，并使用Enron邮件数据集作为受限调查语料库的结构代理。

Conclusion: 本文建立了系统架构基线并概述了严谨的评估方法论，为未来在刑事调查场景中进行实证评估奠定了基础，证明了该架构在安全性和可扩展性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Complex criminal investigations are often hindered by large volumes of unstructured evidence and by the semantic gap between natural language investigative intent and technical search logic. To address this challenge, we present a design and feasibility study of a cloud-native microservice architecture tailored to private-cloud deployments, contributing to research in secure cloud computing and leveraging modern cloud paradigms under high security and scalability requirements. The proposed system integrates Large Language Models into a "Human-in-Control" workflow that translates natural-language queries into syntactically valid OpenSearch Domain-Specific Language expressions. We describe the implementation of a hybrid retrieval strategy within OpenSearch that combines BM25-based lexical search with nested semantic vector embeddings. The paper focuses on system design and preliminary functional validation, establishing an architectural baseline for future empirical evaluation. Technical feasibility is demonstrated through a functional prototype, and a rigorous evaluation methodology is outlined using the Enron Email Dataset as a structural proxy for restricted investigative corpora.

</details>


### [155] [Optimizing High-Throughput Distributed Data Pipelines for Reproducible Deep Learning at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21275)
*Kashish Mittal,Di Yu,Roozbeh Ketabi,Arushi Arora,Brendon Lapp,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过优化Petastorm数据加载器，解决了大规模深度学习训练中的I/O瓶颈和确定性问题，实现了6倍训练加速和GPU利用率从~15%提升至>60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在海量数据集（数十TB）上训练大规模深度学习模型时，面临着硬件利用率低和训练可复现性差的严峻挑战。通过系统性分析，发现网络I/O和CPU端的数据转换（如PyArrow到NumPy）是限制GPU利用率（低至10-15%）的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种优化架构，结合了推送到工作节点级别的数据转换和基于Fanout-Cache的本地磁盘缓存，以减少跨训练周期的冗余I/O和CPU开销。同时，通过实现专用的轮询分发器和结果队列，以及现代化的随机数生成器（RNG）处理，消除了多工作节点共享队列中的竞态条件，实现了严格确定性的数据加载。

Result: 优化方案实现了6倍的加速，将端到端训练时间从22小时减少到3小时；GPU利用率提高到60%以上；显著降低了运行间的差异，实现了高吞吐量和可复现的大规模模型训练。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过针对性地解决分布式训练中的数据加载瓶颈（I/O、CPU转换、并发竞态），可以大幅提升大规模深度学习训练的效率、资源利用率和可复现性，为TB级数据集的训练提供了一套有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Training massive-scale deep learning models on datasets spanning tens of terabytes presents critical challenges in hardware utilization and training reproducibility. In this paper, we identify and resolve profound data-loading bottlenecks within distributed GPU training pipelines using the Petastorm data loader and Apache Parquet datasets. Through systematic profiling, we demonstrate that network I/O and CPU-bound data transformations (e.g., PyArrow to NumPy) constrain GPU utilization to as low as 10-15%. To address this, we propose an optimized architecture that features push-down worker-level transformations coupled with local-disk caching via Fanout-Cache, minimizing redundant I/O and CPU overhead across training epochs. Furthermore, we eliminate race conditions in multi-worker shared queues by implementing dedicated round-robin ventilator and result queues, alongside modernized RNG handling, achieving strict deterministic data loading. Our optimizations yield a 6x speedup, reducing end-to-end training time from 22 hours to 3 hours, increasing GPU utilization to over 60%, and drastically reducing run-to-run variance, enabling robust, high-throughput, and reproducible large-scale model training.

</details>


### [156] [A Task Decomposition and Planning Framework for Efficient LLM Inference in AI-Enabled WiFi-Offload Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21399)
*Mingqi Han,Xinghua Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LLM推理的用户-边缘协作WiFi分流框架，通过LLM规划器进行任务分解和难度预测，并设计分解感知调度策略，在多用户多边缘WiFi网络中实现了更优的延迟-准确性权衡，平均延迟降低20%，整体奖励提升80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI WiFi分流是一种为资源受限无线设备提供大语言模型（LLM）服务的有前景的方法。然而，与传统边缘计算不同，WiFi上的LLM推理必须同时解决异构模型能力、无线竞争、任务复杂性不确定以及推理任务之间的语义相关性等问题。现有方法难以在这些复杂约束下实现高效的LLM推理卸载。

Method: 提出了一种用户-边缘协作框架，包含一个基于LLM的规划器，不仅执行任务分解，还能推断子任务难度和预期输出token长度，从而更准确地估计异构节点上的执行质量和延迟。基于这些估计，设计了分解感知调度策略，在通信、排队和计算约束下联合优化子任务分配、执行和聚合。支持本地执行、直接卸载到边缘AP或分解为多个子任务进行协作执行。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架在延迟-准确性权衡方面优于仅本地和最近边缘基线方法，平均延迟降低20%，整体奖励提高80%。此外，蒸馏后的轻量级规划器能够接近大型教师模型的性能，同时更适合实际边缘部署。

Conclusion: 该工作成功解决了多用户多边缘WiFi网络中LLM推理卸载的复杂挑战，通过智能任务分解和调度优化实现了显著的性能提升。轻量级规划器的蒸馏使其具备实际部署价值，为资源受限设备上的LLM服务提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: AI WiFi offload is emerging as a promising approach for providing large language model (LLM) services to resource-constrained wireless devices. However, unlike conventional edge computing, LLM inference over WiFi must jointly address heterogeneous model capabilities, wireless contention, uncertain task complexity, and semantic correlation among reasoning tasks. In this paper, we investigate LLM inference offloading in a multi-user multi-edge WiFi network, where each task can be executed locally, directly offloaded to a nearby edge access point (AP), or decomposed into multiple subtasks for collaborative execution across local and edge nodes. To this end, we propose a user-edge collaborative framework with an LLM-based planner that not only performs task decomposition but also infers subtask difficulty and expected output token length, enabling more accurate estimation of execution quality and latency on heterogeneous nodes. Based on these estimates, we further design a decomposition-aware scheduling strategy that jointly optimizes subtask assignment, execution, and aggregation under communication, queuing, and computation constraints. Simulation results show that the proposed framework achieves a better latency-accuracy tradeoff than local-only and nearest-edge baselines, reducing the average latency by $20\%$ and improving the overall reward by $80\%$. Moreover, the distilled lightweight planner approaches the performance of the large teacher model while remaining more suitable for practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [157] [Research on the efficiency of data loading and storage in Data Lakehouse architectures for the formation of analytical data systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21449)
*Ivan Borodii,Halyna Osukhivska*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对比研究了三大主流Data Lakehouse系统（Apache Hudi、Apache Iceberg、Delta Lake）在Apache Spark平台上的数据加载与存储效率。实验结果表明：Delta Lake在加载速度方面表现最优，Apache Iceberg在存储效率和稳定性方面更具优势，而Apache Hudi在本次评估中表现欠佳，但可能在增量更新和流处理场景中具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据湖仓架构的兴起，工程师和架构师面临选择合适Lakehouse系统的挑战。不同系统在数据加载性能和存储效率方面存在差异，缺乏系统性对比研究。本研究旨在通过实证分析，帮助从业者根据数据类型、数据量和系统需求选择最优的Data Lakehouse架构。

Method: 研究使用Apache Spark作为分布式数据处理平台，对Apache Hudi、Apache Iceberg和Delta Lake三个系统进行对比实验。设计了四个顺序ETL流程，涵盖数据读取、转换和加载。测试数据包括结构化数据（CSV）和半结构化数据（JSON），文件大小达到7GB。评估指标包括数据加载时间和文件系统中表占用的磁盘空间大小。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) Delta Lake在各类数据量的加载速度方面表现最优，适合对加载性能要求高的系统；(2) Apache Iceberg在稳定性和磁盘空间节省方面表现最佳，适合对存储效率要求严格的场景；(3) Apache Hudi在数据加载和存储评估任务中效率较低，但可能在增量更新和流处理场景中发挥作用。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统性地对比了Apache Iceberg、Apache Hudi和Delta Lake的性能与存储特性，为构建分析型数据系统选择合适的Lakehouse架构提供了实证依据。研究成果帮助数据工程师和架构师在加载性能与存储效率之间做出权衡，根据具体业务需求选择最优架构方案。

Abstract: The paper presents a study of the efficiency of loading and storing data in the three most common Data Lakehouse systems, including Apache Hudi, Apache Iceberg, and Delta Lake, using Apache Spark as a distributed data processing platform. The study analyzes the behavior of each system when processing structured (CSV) and semi-structured (JSON) data of different sizes, including loading files up to 7 GB in size. The purpose of the work is to determine the most optimal Data Lakehouse architecture based on the type and volume of data sources, data loading performance using Apache Spark, and disk size of data for forming analytical data systems. The research covers the development of four sequential ETL processes, which include reading, transforming, and loading data into tables in each of the Data Lakehouse systems. The efficiency of each Lakehouse was evaluated according to two key criteria: data loading time and the volume of tables formed in the file system. For the first time, a comparison of performance and data storage in Apache Iceberg, Apache Hudi, and Delta Lake Data Lakehouse systems was conducted to select the most relevant architecture for building analytical data systems. The practical value of the study consists in the fact that it assists data engineers and architects in choosing the most appropriate Lakehouse architecture, understanding the balance between loading performance and storage efficiency. Experimental results showed that Delta Lake is the most optimal architecture for systems where the priority is the speed of loading data of any volume, while Apache Iceberg is most appropriate for systems where stability and disk space savings are critical. Apache Hudi proved ineffective in data loading and storage evaluation tasks but could potentially be effective in incremental update and streaming processing scenarios.

</details>


### [158] [Risk-Aware and Stable Edge Server Selection Under Network Latency SLOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21483)
*Mohan Liyanage,Arnova Abdullah,Eldiyar Zhantileuov,Rolf Schuster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级可解释的动态边缘服务器选择框架，通过显式考虑尾部风险和切换稳定性，在保持低延迟的同时显著降低截止时间错过率和切换频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在延迟敏感的边缘计算应用中，现有的服务器选择方法往往只关注平均延迟，忽略了尾部风险（即服务级目标SLO违规风险）和切换稳定性问题。短期网络波动会导致服务器选择振荡，增加切换开销并影响服务质量。因此需要一种能够平衡性能、风险和稳定性的决策框架。

Method: 该方法为每个候选服务器计算网络延迟的预测均值和不确定性摘要。使用紧致正态近似结合保守的Cantelli界来评估SLO违规风险，实现风险感知的服务器选择。引入基于百分位的评分机制和滞回控制来稳定决策，抑制短期网络波动引起的振荡性切换。

Result: 在多服务器边缘测试平台上的实验表明，在严格的SLO（τ=0.5s）约束下，相比仅基于均值的基线方法，该方法将截止时间错过率从39%降至34%，同时将切换频率从46%降至5.5%（约88%的降低），并维持平均延迟在SLO以下（约0.45s）。

Conclusion: 显式的风险评估结合保稳控制机制能够实现动态边缘环境中实用且鲁棒的自适应服务器选择，有效平衡了性能、风险和稳定性。

Abstract: We present a lightweight and interpretable decision framework for dynamic edge server selection in latency-critical applications that explicitly accounts for tail risk and switching stability. Each candidate server is characterised by predictive mean and uncertainty summaries of network latency, which are used to estimate the risk of service-level objective (SLO) violations and to guide selection. Risk is evaluated using a tight Normal approximation complemented by a conservative Cantelli bound, while percentile-based scoring coupled with hysteresis stabilizes decisions and suppresses oscillatory switching under short-lived network fluctuations.
  Experimental results on a multi-server edge testbed with a strict SLO of $τ= 0.5$\,s show that the proposed approach reduces the deadline-miss rate from 39\% to 34\% compared to a mean-only baseline, while reducing switching frequency from 46\% to 5.5\% ($\approx$88\% reduction) and maintaining sub-SLO average latency ($\approx$0.45\,s). These results demonstrate that explicit risk evaluation combined with stability-preserving control enables practical and robust adaptive server selection in dynamic edge environments.

</details>


### [159] [Systematizing Blockchain Research Themes and Design Patterns: Insights from the University Blockchain Research Initiative (UBRI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21517)
*Chien-Chih Chen,Yitian Wang,Emma Nasseri,Yebo Feng,Lauren Weymouth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以UBRI网络为案例，研究了区块链学术研究向可部署系统和监管框架转化的制度机制与协调模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链和数字资产生态系统的快速扩张加剧了将学术研究转化为可部署系统和监管框架的挑战。虽然密码学、共识机制、数字资产和治理方面的进步显著，但在生态系统规模上维持研究到部署转化的制度机制仍然相对缺乏理论化研究。

Method: 以大学区块链研究倡议（UBRI）网络作为长期学术与行业合作的代表性案例，基于2022年至2025年的研究成果和会议资料进行分析，抽象出连接研究贡献与部署约束及政策适应的系统级主题。

Result: 综合提炼出技术和制度领域中的重复设计张力，包括可扩展性与安全性的权衡、去中心化与治理的权衡、隐私与合规的权衡，并提供了理解学术研究如何影响生产架构、监管发展和新兴去中心化基础设施生态系统韧性的结构化视角。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了支持研究向部署转化的架构和协调模式，为理解新兴去中心化基础设施中学术研究如何指导生产架构和监管发展提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of blockchain and digital asset ecosystems has intensified the challenge of translating academic research into deployable systems and regulatory frameworks. While advances in cryptography, consensus, digital assets, and governance are substantial, institutional mechanisms that sustain research-to-deployment translation at ecosystem scale remain comparatively under-theorized. This paper examines the architectural and coordination patterns that enable such translation, using the University Blockchain Research Initiative (UBRI) network as a representative case of long-term academic and industry collaboration. Drawing on research outputs and convenings from 2022 to 2025, we synthesize recurring design tensions across technical and institutional domains, including scalability versus security, decentralization versus governance, and privacy versus compliance. Rather than cataloging individual projects, we abstract system-level themes that connect research contributions to deployment constraints and policy adaptation, providing a structured lens for understanding how academic research informs production architectures, regulatory development, and ecosystem resilience in emerging decentralized infrastructures.

</details>


### [160] [Leveraging SIMD for Accelerating Large-number Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21566)
*Subhrajit Das,Abhishek Bichhawat,Yuvraj Patel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DigitsOnTurbo (DoT)，一种通过重构大数运算算法以适配SIMD并行性的方法，相比传统方法实现了显著加速，并在实际应用中取得了可观的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数运算在科学计算和密码学中应用广泛，但由于传统算法固有的数据依赖性，限制了其在现代CPU上利用SIMD（单指令多数据）并行计算的能力。现有方法难以有效向量化，因此亟需一种新的计算范式来释放SIMD在大数运算中的潜力。

Method: 提出DigitsOnTurbo (DoT)方法，核心在于改变计算结构，围绕独立的数据并行操作重构计算流程，而非简单地对标准算法进行向量化适配，从而消除依赖瓶颈并充分利用SIMD指令集的优势。

Result: 在SIMD实现层面，DoT在加法和减法上实现高达1.85倍加速，乘法达到2.3倍加速。集成到最先进的库中后，加法和减法加速高达4倍，乘法加速高达2倍。在端到端应用中，科学计算吞吐量提升达19.3%，密码学实现的延迟降低7.9%，吞吐量提升5.9%。

Conclusion: 通过重构大数运算的计算范式以适配数据并行性，DoT成功突破了传统算法在SIMD架构上的性能瓶颈，为科学计算和密码学应用提供了显著的性能增益，证明了算法结构性优化对硬件特性适配的重要性。

Abstract: Large-number arithmetic, widely used in scientific computing and cryptography, has seen limited adoption of single instruction, multiple data (SIMD) parallelism on modern CPUs due to the inherent dependencies in traditional algorithms. We present DigitsOnTurbo (DoT), which restructures the computation around independent, data-parallel operations, rather than vectorizing the standard algorithms, thereby leveraging the benefits provided by SIMD. Over prior SIMD implementations, DoT achieves up to 1.85x speedups for addition and subtraction, and 2.3x for multiplication. When integrated into state-of-the-art libraries, DoT yields up to 4x speedup for addition and subtraction, and up to 2x speedup for multiplication, cascading into end-to-end throughput gains of up to 19.3% for scientific computations, and up to 7.9% latency and 5.9% throughput improvements on cryptographic implementations.

</details>
